- E38 (calibration switches): 45 switches that lived only in config.py / .env are now on the Real Money Calibration page (real-trade +21, position-stocks +24: gates, caps, risk per trade, A11 / A12 limits, stop protections), all read at call time and checked by `check_control_catalog.py`, no default changed. New real switch `JOURNAL_NOTES_ENABLED` for E30 (routes refuse with 409 when off; UI says so). Guard test `test_e37_catalog_coverage.py` in both services fails when a new `*_ENABLED` / `*_MODE` setting is not on the page or exempt with a reason. Doc: `docs/E38_CALIBRATION_SWITCHES.md`.
- E37 (E30, journal notes): every Position Stocks trade can carry a reason (<= 300 characters) and a feeling (calm, confident, hopeful, unsure, impatient, fomo, fear, greed, revenge). New table `scalp_trade_journal` (own snapshot of the trade, so notes survive the 3-day retention cleanup), `orders/journal_notes.py`, routes `GET /journal/notes` and `PUT /journal/notes/{position_id}` (admin), optional `reason` / `emotion` on `POST /positions/manual/buy` (validated before the order; a note failure never fails a placed buy). Frontend: Feeling + Why boxes on Manual buy, and Board tab cards *Win rate by feeling* and *Trade notes*. Descriptive only, nothing gates on it. Tests: 12 backend (`tests/test_e30_journal_notes.py`), 2 frontend node tests (`journalNotesView.test.mjs`). Doc: `docs/E30_JOURNAL_NOTES.md`.
- E36 (E29, pattern alerts): one daily digest (after the close, `PATTERN_ALERTS_TIME` 16:05 IST, weekdays) of fresh chart signals on the WATCHLIST: confirmed breakout / breakdown, golden / death cross (last 2 daily bars), Bollinger squeeze on / released. Alert only, nothing trades on it, and the message says the signals are unproven. A signal already sent is not repeated for 3-10 days (state in `/data/pattern_alerts_state.json`, marked only after the notification service accepted the digest). New `scheduler/pattern_alerts.py`, `GET /scheduler/pattern-alerts/status`, `POST /scheduler/pattern-alerts/run?dry_run=true`. `chart_context` gained an uncapped additive `events` list (the 14-badge cap could hide a cross or squeeze on a crowded chart). 11 scheduler tests + 1 shared test (+ generated copy). Doc: `docs/E29_PATTERN_ALERTS.md`.
- E35 (C21, log-only pattern logging): the C18-C20 pattern signals now get their own fixed feature row (`PATTERN_COLUMNS`, 23 `pt_*` columns, own hash) and a shadow evidence record (`scoring.pattern_shadow`, five separate groups, `in_tqs: false`), both returned by `analyze_extras()`. `FEATURE_COLUMNS_V2` and `technical_quality_score` are unchanged (tested); no gate, score or model reads the new rows. Copies re-synced; 8 new tests (+ generated service copy). A7 re-checked: the general 14:00 entry cutoff already exists (groups 470 / 479). Doc: `docs/C21_PATTERN_LOGGING.md`.
- E27 (candles on every timeframe): the candle chart now supports 1m, 5m, 15m, 30m, 1h and daily, backend and frontend. New `shared/market_structure/timeframes.py` (session-aligned buckets, closed vs forming candle, IST dates, session-break rule for 2-3 candle patterns), `chart_context(tf=...)`, technical `/chart-context/{sym}?tf=` (daily path unchanged; intraday misses answer 200 + reason), gateway `?tf=` with its own `technical_chart` breaker, market-data `/history` intraday row cap (`MAX_HISTORY_ROWS_INTRADAY`, default 2600; the 260 cap cut a 1-minute chart to 260 minutes). Frontend: timeframe selector + per-timeframe zoom, time axis, session separators, hollow forming candle, auto-refresh in market hours. 40 new Python tests (plus generated service copies) + E27 block in `chartContext.test.mjs`. Doc: `docs/E27_CANDLE_TIMEFRAMES.md`.
- 2026-10-22 (entry live anchor): position-stocks now moves the stop / target legs to the freshest live tick at order time (same stop % / target %), instead of the scan price. IONEXCHANG: signal 354.60, fill 352.30, stop 0.15 % under the fill, out in 21 s. `ENTRY_ANCHOR_TO_LIVE=0` = old behaviour. New `orders/live_anchor.py`, catalog flag, autocalib-protected, 7 tests. Doc: `docs/ENTRY_LIVE_ANCHOR_2026-10-22.md`.
- group491 (atomic file): an interrupted walk-forward no longer empties `backtest.json`. `vm_calibrate.sh` and the runner's `out_file` jobs now write `<file>.part` and replace the saved file only when the command finished, so a runner restart mid-run (21 Oct `calibration_full`, cut off at 15/355 symbols) keeps the last good `backtest.json` instead of leaving it empty. 3 new tests. Doc: `docs/GROUP491_ATOMIC_BACKTEST_JSON.md`.
- group491 (resume): the 2-hour structure walk-forward (`scripts/structure_backtest.py`) now saves each finished symbol (`--checkpoint`) and continues a killed / timed-out run (`--resume`), with `--jobs N`; runner job `structure_backtest` is resumable (Resume button, `ckpt/structure_<seed>`) and `vm_calibrate.sh` passes the flags (`RESUME=1`). Horizon / min-n / thresholds changes reuse the saved symbols; a changed history file, step, entry or costs recompute. +7 tests, group466 test updated. Doc: `docs/GROUP491_STRUCTURE_WALKFORWARD_RESUME.md`.
- group490: CI + runner fixes. (1) `RT_BREAKER_PROBE_ALERT_REPEAT_S` read was not blank-safe (failed `test_env_numeric_sweep`); fixed + test. (2) circuit_breaker shared-copy drift accepted in the baseline on purpose (only real-trade alerts via Telegram). (3) `calibration_pack.py` crashed with a JSONDecodeError on an empty `backtest.json` (a walk-forward that did not finish): the script now ends with a clear line and the runner reports an empty `.json` input as "needs data first"; +5 tests. (4) calibration-runner `test_every_rule_points_at_a_real_switch...` (failing since group 481) now accepts protected keys read from service config. Doc: `docs/GROUP490_CI_AND_PACK_FIXES.md`.
- group489: audit fix to group487. The small-leftover gate left out every leftover without a recent cached price, including symbols missing from Dhan's scrip map that only yfinance can price (they would never have been priced through `/quotes/bulk` again). Those now keep the yfinance path (`_dhan_can_price`, fails safe to the old path); only symbols Dhan knows but answered late are skipped. +3 tests (16 in `test_group487_bulk_small_leftover.py`). Doc: `docs/GROUP487_BULK_SMALL_LEFTOVER.md`.
- group488: position-stocks' first background refresh of a stale scrip-master snapshot now waits `ANGELONE_SCRIP_MASTER_BG_START_DELAY_S` (default 30 s + up to 50 % jitter from process start) so it does not hit AngelOne's file server together with market-data-service after a compose up; cold starts are not delayed. The snapshot volumes (`scrip-cache-md`, `scrip-cache-ps`) were already in compose. 8 new tests. Doc: `docs/GROUP488_SCRIP_MASTER_STAGGER.md`.
- group487: `/quotes/bulk` sent the few symbols Dhan could not price to `yf.download` (18 s hard timeout, then reported as a 502 rate-limit event). When Dhan priced >= 90 % of the request and <= 8 symbols are left, those get a cached price (<= 180 s, tagged `stale_leftover(<source>)`) or are left out, with no yfinance call; indices and held symbols keep the old path. A yfinance hard timeout / open circuit is no longer reported as a rate limit (real 429 still is). `QUOTE_BULK_SMALL_LEFTOVER_SKIP=0` restores the old behaviour. 13 new tests; market-data suite 1726 passed. Doc: `docs/GROUP487_BULK_SMALL_LEFTOVER.md`.
- group485: real-trade circuit breaker sent a Telegram "probe failed again" every 120 s for the whole outage (58 in a row on 2026-10-21). First failed probe still alerts; repeats now once per `RT_BREAKER_PROBE_ALERT_REPEAT_S` (default 1800, 0 = old behaviour); alerts and `/resilience/status` now include `last_error`. 26 breaker tests pass; ENV_REFERENCE regenerated. Doc: `docs/GROUP485_RT_BREAKER_ALERT_THROTTLE.md`.
- group470: fixes from the 19-20 Oct runner outputs (no gate behavior changed). (1) a full admin JWT was printed in `calibration_full` output: the redactor now hides any JWT, `--token VALUE` and `TOKEN=` values. (2) `gate_check` and `calibration_pack` failed with exit 2: `--ab` is now repeated per file (`globflag:` argv token). (3) `calibration_snapshot` / checkpointed jobs reported `input missing` for the folder they create (`--store` / `--checkpoint` are output flags). (4) `monday_check` no longer FAILs the session verdict outside market hours. (5) `tsfm_nightly` / `tsfm_probe` skip cleanly when the worker is not started. (6) G1 footer names the failing check; one G1 budget (15 ms, `G1_BUDGET_MS`). (7) slippage line states its sign. `docs/GROUP470_RUN_OUTPUT_FIXES.md`.
- group463: overall UI polish (frontend only): skip link, per-tab ErrorBoundary around the page area, toast live region, shared EmptyState, sticky/zebra tables, card depth, themed scrollbars. design-v4 tests 6 pass; syntax-only tsc clean. Doc: `docs/GROUP463_UI_POLISH.md`.
- group462: phase 4 finished (frontend only): price roll on the Surprise Stocks price column, lazy painting of Watchlist tiles. Spinners inside action buttons stay by design. Syntax-only tsc clean; design-v4 tests 5 pass. Doc: `docs/GROUP461_UI_PHASES_3_TO_5.md`.
- group461: login redesign phases 3-5 (frontend only). REAL / DEMO frame with a spelled-out ribbon on Real Auto Trade, Position Stocks and Real Money Calibration; price roll + tick glint wired into the live price rows (prices only, never P&L or order values); Market Movers / sentiment header skeletons and keyboard-operable cards; focus ring, tap targets, TV / reduced-motion / high-contrast rules, lazy painting of long lists. Test: `frontend/src/design-v4.test.mjs` (5 pass, in `npm test`). Not run here: npm ci / full tsc / vite build (no node_modules); syntax-only tsc clean. Doc: `docs/GROUP461_UI_PHASES_3_TO_5.md`.
- group459: Daily close pack "Telegram error: URLError". The runner could not reach notification-scheduler-service (refused / DNS / timeout) and tried exactly once, then showed only the exception class. `notify.send` now retries connection failures, timeouts and HTTP 502/503/504 (3 tries, 4 s apart; 4xx is final) and the note names the cause and the URL, e.g. `error: URLError: [Errno 111] Connection refused reaching http://notification-scheduler-service:8000 (after 3 tries; check ...)`. A failed Telegram message still never changes the run's status. Test: `test_send_retries_a_connection_failure_and_says_why`. NOTE: this only fixes the lost message; the pack's own `failed` status is a separate problem (see its run output in Runs & downloads).
- group458: CI repair (every red check from the last run). (1) `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.md` regenerated (`scripts/gen_env_reference.py`, 734 variables; `--check` passes). (2) position-stocks `tests/test_group309_board_runner_http.py`: the pinned `/board` route list now includes the existing `GET /board/intraday/{symbol}` (read-only, no admin / commit, as the test requires). (3) The pinned silent-`except: pass` counts (group 322) are NOT raised: the 4 new silent excepts in position-stocks `orders/pullback_rearm.py` (2) and `orders/entry.py` (2) and the 1 in real-trade `entry_engine/entry.py` (rearm requeue rollback) now log (debug, or warning where arming fails) instead of hiding the error. (4) real-trade tests `test_below_min_price_rejects` (x2) and `test_price_floor_is_judged_before_history` used a price of 15.0 / 10.6, which is above the floor since CANDIDATE_MIN_STOCK_PRICE went 20 -> 10; they now use 8.0 / 8.6 (code unchanged, `reject_kind: price_floor` was already returned). (5) calibration-runner-service: Docker reads `Dockerfile.dockerignore` (build context = repo root), which lacked the secret / cache rules of the root `.dockerignore`, so a local `.env` would have been sent to the build; they are added, and `test_dockerignore.py` now reads the file Docker really uses and checks that every COPY source of a repo-root build survives it. Not run here: the Python suites (no pytest / PyPI in this sandbox); the edited tests' logic was checked with plain Python, the dockerignore guard test ran in full (90 pass).
- group457: Real Money Calibration - (1) the admin session is a FIXED window from login (30 min default; `issue_session_token` never extends it) although the login card said "30 idle minutes", so a long run logged you out in the middle of watching it with no warning. The text is corrected and the page now shows a warning in the last 10 minutes (`SessionNotice`, `sessionSecondsLeft()` in calibrationApi.ts) saying the run keeps going on the server and where to find it after logging in again. (2) Connection card inputs are full width on phones (were fixed 18rem, overflowed); (3) Daily trades day rows work from the keyboard (Tab, Enter / Space); (4) Runs list buttons no longer squeeze. Session length itself is unchanged (SESSION_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MINUTES).
- group456: Real Money Calibration UI - (1) ROOT CAUSE of "the run window is not visible, page dimmed, no close button": `design-v3.css` animated every page wrapper with fill-mode `both`, which keeps a transform on it, so the popup's `position: fixed` was measured against the whole tall page, not the screen (dim layer over the content only, window in the middle of the page). Fill-mode is now `backwards`, and `Modal` is drawn into `document.body` (portal), so every popup in the app is fixed. (2) a job now says RUNNING vs IN QUEUE (it said "In queue" for both), with a live strip (started X ago, time limit), Watch and Cancel buttons; (3) a floating "Running: ... Watch / Cancel" pill stays on screen while a run is going; the banner chip is a Watch button; (4) options panel has a header and a close (x), checkboxes sit inline; (5) Run / Watch / Cancel and Run-plan buttons form one aligned column (`.run-actions`, stacks on phones); (6) toasts clear themselves. Test: `src/popupLayout.test.mjs`.
- group455: unused-import cleanup (second batch, no behavior change) - 57 unused names removed from 33 files (training / prediction / decision / fundamental / calibration-runner / scripts). Only imports that nothing uses, that no test or other module references (`module.name`, quoted patch targets, `from module import name`), that are top level, carry no `# noqa`, and are NOT in a shared-copy module (oracle_compat, kv_cache, rate_limiter, ... and `market_structure/engine.py`). `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.md` line numbers regenerated.
- group454: gateway CORS - `allow_origins=["*"]` plus a second middleware that forced `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` on every response meant nothing could narrow it. New `services/api-gateway/cors_policy.py` + `GATEWAY_CORS_ORIGINS` (comma separated origins): set = only those origins get CORS headers, the 500 handler echoes only an allowed origin, and `/ws` closes (1008) other origins; unset = wildcard exactly as before (boot warning), so an existing deploy does not break on rebuild. `allow_credentials` is now False (the app uses a Bearer header, never cookies). Added to docker-compose.yml and .env.oracle.example. Other services' CORS left as is (see docs/GROUP454_GATEWAY_CORS.md). Tests: `tests/test_cors_policy.py`.
- group453: gateway login - the api-gateway had no login on its dangerous routes (nginx publishes it at /api/), so anyone reaching the domain could redirect the alert channels, power the app off, start training or wipe the data feed. 14 routes (`/notifications/{config,config/{channel},call/me,test,send-picks}`, `/training/train`, `/ops/{power-off,circuit-reset,overnight/config,overnight/run,resume-activity}`, the three `hard-reset` aliases) now need the existing admin session token (`Authorization: Bearer`, JWT HS256 with SESSION_SECRET) via `services/api-gateway/admin_guard.py`. Fails closed: no SESSION_SECRET on the gateway = 503. Kill switch `GATEWAY_ADMIN_GUARD=false`. `SESSION_SECRET` + `GATEWAY_ADMIN_GUARD` added to the api-gateway block of docker-compose.yml. Frontend `src/gatewayAuth.ts` adds the stored real-trade token to those paths only. Tests: `tests/test_admin_guard.py`, `src/gatewayAuth.test.mjs`. Log in on the Real Trade tab first, or these buttons answer 401.
- group446: four recovered fixes plus the service ON / OFF advice. (1) control kit (both copies): a float switch no longer shows PENDING RESTART for `3` vs `3.0` (both sides compared canonical). (2) session_proof: after a restart past 10:00 the first later price is a late baseline and a different later price proves `open` (never `closed`), so the 10:15 live check stops failing after a late restart; new status field `late_baseline_symbols`. (3) control_snapshot: switch titles cut at 60 instead of 44. (4) calibration runner: a missing input is `attention` ("needs data first"), not FAILED. NEW `scripts/service_onoff_advice.py` + job `service_advice` (second step of the Daily close pack, Mon-Fri 16:05 IST): KEEP ON / WATCH / TURN OFF / NOT ENOUGH DATA per service from its real closed trades, overlap on the shared Dhan account, better service if only one can run; RECOMMENDS ONLY, never changes a switch. 446b (after the first live run): the advice no longer counts a trading day still in progress (run before 15:45 IST) as a losing day / streak day / trading day (shown in a NOTE; `--include-today` overrides), and has `--since YYYY-MM-DD` (job field "Judge only from date") to judge only the days after a switch change. `daily_trades` entry time left unchanged: it matches the real BUY fill (no bug found). Docs: docs/GROUP446_FIXES_AND_SERVICE_ADVICE.md. Tests: 3 + 1 + 1 + 1 updated + 7 new, all pass (pytest).

A trading-service URL that was never set (empty) sent that tab's calls to the page's own origin or the other service. `deriveServiceUrls()` now fills a missing URL from the gateway URL / the other service (`/realtrade`, `/positionstocks`) and saves it; a URL already set is never changed. The Switches tab refuses to show switches of the wrong service (checks `service` in /control/flags and says which URL to fix); the Connection card now shows and edits all three URLs. Frontend only: rebuild the `frontend` container.

Fixes: frontend `call()` now rejects a non-JSON answer with a clear message (names the missing nginx route); null-safe reads in RunnerContext / RunPlanPanel / JobCard / RunsPanel / TestsPanel; new `components/ui/ErrorBoundary.tsx` around the tab (a render error shows a card, not a blank app); `frontend/nginx.conf` now proxies `/calibration/` to calibration-runner-service:8012 itself (works even if the host nginx block is missing). Rebuild the `frontend` container.

G5-G8 / F3 do not exist in the repo and are not invented. Doc: `docs/GROUP442_REAL_MONEY_CALIBRATION_CONTROL.md`.
- group411: broker-stop retry storm fixed - the log showed ABDL (CNC, 6 qty) re-sent to Dhan every sync pass as `STOP_LOSS_MARKET` ("Trigger Price should be greater than Price") then `STOP_LOSS` ("Insufficient Holding Quantity" = CDSL eDIS / TPIN), forever, with only the alert throttled. `execution/broker_stop.py`: (1) an eDIS / TPIN refusal now backs that symbol off for `BROKER_STOP_EDIS_BACKOFF_S` (default 900 s, 0 = old behaviour) and then tries once more, so it self-heals after 'Verify Holdings' and stops hitting Dhan (and the blocking SDK call on the exit loop) every pass; (2) once the STOP_LOSS retry gets past the trigger validation, later placements in the process go straight to `STOP_LOSS` (one Dhan call, not two); (3) a configured `STOP_LOSS` that hits the trigger-price error is no longer re-sent unchanged; (4) the alert now says to open the Dhan app -> Verify Holdings. Backed-off passes are counted in the sync tally as `backoff`. The eDIS authorisation itself cannot be done in code (TPIN is typed on CDSL's page). New tests in `tests/test_group322_broker_stop.py`.
- group389: position-stocks entry price guard fails closed on a missing tick - `orders/entry.py` `_price_guard_reject` skipped the stale-tick and slippage checks when the symbol had NO tick at all, so a MARKET entry could go out with no live price to compare against. New `ENTRY_REQUIRE_LIVE_TICK` (default on, `0` restores the old behaviour) skips it with reason `PRICE_GUARD:NO_LIVE_TICK`; the manual BUY path (`_stale_tick_reject`) is unchanged (a human confirmed that price). conftest pins it off for the older suite; 6 new tests in `tests/test_entry.py`; documented in `.env.example`, `.env.oracle.recommended`, `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.md`. position-stocks suite 3762 passed.
- group388: stale-price gate fails closed - real-trade `entry_engine/entry.py` `_entry_quote_age_reason`: `ENTRY_QUOTE_AGE_UNKNOWN` now defaults to `refuse` (was `allow`), so an entry is never built on a price whose age nobody can state (market-data row without `age_s`/`as_of`, garbage timestamp); the candidate is re-read once through the priority lane and held like an old price, then consumed as a WAIT after `ENTRY_QUOTE_AGE_HOLD_MIN`. Rollback: `ENTRY_QUOTE_AGE_UNKNOWN=allow`. `.env.example`, `.env.oracle.recommended`, `docs/GROUP290_*`, `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.md` updated; group290 tests flipped + rollback tests added. real-trade suite 4703 passed + 1 skipped. Same fail-closed default the watchlist baseline check already had.

session, dated). This file is just an index so they're discoverable
without 50+ files cluttering the repo root.

In the 13:39 IST boot log a `getCandleData` 403 started the single group 211 cooldown, and every quote caller then logged `AngelOne-first did not price X (global AngelOne cooldown (403) is running)` while quotes had been answering normally (`AngelOne REST resolved 89/89`) right before. The feed stopped after 52 of 499 tokens, `/quote` fell to yfinance (18 s hard timeouts, "rate-limit bucket saturated"), only 306 of 653 symbols were priced and about 50 real-trade `/quote` calls ReadTimed out. `angelone_budget.py` now keeps the cooldown per family: `candle` (getCandleData) and `quote` (ltpData, quote batches, feed poll, gainers). A candle 403 pauses candle callers only, a quote 403 pauses quote callers only; each family escalates (30 s -> 60 s) and suppresses late answers on its own. `angelone_client.py` (`get_candles` checks the candle family, the rest the quote family) and `angelone_ws_feed.py` (poll cycle, mid-cycle stop and the HTTP-denied safety net use the quote family). `ANGELONE_SPLIT_COOLDOWN=0` restores the shared cooldown. `GET /angelone/budget` gains `split_cooldown` and per-family `cooldowns` (old keys kept; they now mean "any family"). Updated tests that asserted the old behaviour (group 211: 6, group 225: 1). Items 3 and 4 of the review are still open. Market-data suite here: 1079 passed. Doc: docs/GROUP228_SPLIT_ANGELONE_COOLDOWN.md. Tests: market-data tests/test_group227_split_cooldown.py (new, 34).

The same symbols (AXISCADES, RAYMOND, TRANSRAIL, JAYKAY, INDHOTEL, AETHER, KAVDEFENCE, DELTACORP, NYKAA ...) were rejected for stable reasons ("top 12% of 52w range", "6m return < -10%") and re-evaluated every 3 minutes at 7 `/history` calls + a quote + a market-cap call each - the biggest candle-load driver behind the AngelOne 403 cooldown. `candidate_engine/candidates.py`: `_multi_tf_analysis` tags every definite rejection with `reject_kind`; a per-symbol cache remembers them (`REJECT_CACHE_STABLE_S`=3600 for price floor / 6m downtrend / ATR cap / volume health, `REJECT_CACHE_PRICE_S`=900 for weighted bullish score / 52w range / near resistance; both 0 = off). Cached symbols skip the quote prefetch, all 7 history calls and the market-cap call, and log at DEBUG; one INFO line per cycle counts them. Never cached: data-starved, incomplete-history, no-quote, errors, passes; entries are dropped at the IST date change and when the adaptive ATR cap rises above the cached ATR. Also found while tracing: `5d` was missing from market-data's AngelOne and NSE period maps, so the candidate engine's "1w" request silently pulled 180 days whenever AngelOne served the candles (a 6-month return labelled 1 week); it is now 7 days and `5d` is never widened by `MAX_HISTORY_PERIOD`. Items 1 and 3 of the same review are NOT done. Real-trade suite here: 3533 passed, 1 skipped, 1 error (the group172 teardown error, identical on the unmodified upload); market-data 1045 passed. The range gate, adaptive levels and screener use the exchange's day high/low from the mode-3 frame instead of the 65-minute tick buffer. New no-follow-through exit (no +0.5% in 20 min), stagnation 45 -> 30 min, bar-ATR levels on by default, breakeven trigger capped at 1%, 1m and 15m windows paused (`DISABLED_SCAN_WINDOWS`). At the open VINCOFE, SATIN, KOHINOOR, COMSYN, KKCL and DCI each hit IndianAPI repeatedly and got 429 every time: `fundamental/indianapi_fallback.py` remembered nothing, so every call took a rate-limit slot, made a request and logged an ERROR. A 429 (status code or via `raise_for_status`) now pauses ALL IndianAPI requests for 120 s, doubling per 429 in a row up to 900 s (`Retry-After` honoured when longer, capped); other failures keep only that symbol out for 10 min; a success resets the streak. Fresh and stale cache are still served during a cooldown. One WARNING per 429 instead of an ERROR per symbol.   It only compared 2026 dates, so 2027 dates added to one of the seven copies and forgotten in another would never be reported, and on 1 Jan 2027 every service would silently treat holidays as trading days. Now compares every date from 2026 on and prints a NOTE (exit 0) when the current or next year has no dates (from 1 Oct) - it prints that today. No 2027 dates added (no official calendar). +10 tests (28 pass in the file under a stand-in runner). No service code changed. Details: `docs/GROUP142_HOLIDAY_SYNC_SCRIPT_ALL_YEARS.md`.
- 2026-10-04 (group 141)  Scan-universe cache TTL treats an NSE holiday as closed (api-gateway).
  `_build_scan_universe` used weekday + hours only, so on a weekday holiday the universe was cached 30 min instead of 6 h and rebuilt all day. Now 6 h on a holiday; lookup failure keeps the old answer. The other weekday-only checks group 140 listed were reviewed: surprise_scanner and data_feed already check holidays, the rest are safe by design (see the doc). Test table row for Fri 2026-10-02 corrected (it was a holiday), +1 test; not run under pytest here, real TTL block verified by extraction. Rebuild api-gateway. Details: `docs/GROUP141_SCAN_UNIVERSE_TTL_HOLIDAY.md`.
- 2026-10-04 (group 140)  NSE holiday awareness in three more places (analysis-intelligence technical, decision, position-stocks WS).
  Same gap as group 139: the technical cache TTL, the decision cache TTL and the position AngelOne WebSocket idle check only looked at weekday + hours, so on a weekday holiday (Fri 2026-10-02, Tue 2026-10-20) they behaved as open. Each now returns closed/idle on a holiday (off switch and lookup failure keep the old behaviour). `check_holiday_lists_sync.py` now checks seven copies. 9 new tests; the decision ones run here, the technical and position ones could not (no httpx/numpy/websockets) - function bodies verified by extraction. Rebuild those three services. Details: `docs/GROUP140_HOLIDAY_AWARENESS_TECHNICAL_DECISION_POSITION_FEED.md`.

New `_NSE_HOLIDAYS_2026` + `is_nse_holiday_ist()` in `market_hours.py`; `is_feed_window_ist()` and `main.is_market_open()` return closed on a holiday (always-on switch still wins; lookup failure = old behaviour). `scripts/check_holiday_lists_sync.py` now checks five copies. 18 new tests + one test-helper base-week fix; run here under a stand-in runner only (no pytest/fastapi). Rebuild market-data-service. Details: `docs/GROUP139_MARKET_DATA_NSE_HOLIDAY_AWARENESS.md`.
- 2026-10-04 (group 138)  Closed market: `scan(cached=true)` serves a result computed after the last close instead of re-sweeping (api-gateway).
  The boot no longer swept when closed, but every later cached call age-rejected the restored result (~220 s) and ran the full ~1000-quote sweep again. Now, on weekends/holidays/outside 08:30-15:30 IST, a result at least 600 s past the last trading-day close is served (`market_closed_cache: true`); pre-open stays live; per-symbol requests excluded; `SURPRISE_CLOSED_MARKET_CACHE=0` turns it off. 12 new tests, api-gateway suite 8208 passed. Rebuild api-gateway. Details: `docs/GROUP138_CLOSED_MARKET_CACHED_SCAN_SERVES_POST_CLOSE_RESULT.md`.
- 2026-10-04 (group 137)  An early `scan(cached=True)` call can no longer delete the saved last result (api-gateway).
  `scan(cached=True)` loaded the durable last result with the plain kv read, which DELETES an expired row, so a caller arriving before the closed-market boot warm (20 s after start) destroyed the only copy and forced the full quote sweep. It now uses the stale-aware loader; the age check still decides freshness. 2 new tests (real kv_cache + SQL table; both fail on the old line), api-gateway suite 8196 passed. Rebuild api-gateway. Details: `docs/GROUP137_EARLY_CACHED_SCAN_KEEPS_LAST_RESULT.md`.

  to the time left, the enrichment fan-out uses `asyncio.wait` so finished calls are kept and slow ones cancelled (was
  `gather`, which could outlive the budget), and Gemini is skipped (template) with < 3 s left or cut off at the deadline;
  (4) a timeout that survives the retry now returns an honest HOLD ("taking longer than usual ... try again in a minute",

- 2026-10-03 (group 55)  #13 item (continued from group 54): the same `weight > capacity` flaw in the four other token
  buckets, all fixed the same way. Tokens are capped at `capacity`, so `tokens >= weight` can never become true for a
  heavier weight; a plain `acquire()` slept out the whole `max_wait` on every call and then "proceeded anyway".
  Files: `market-data-service/rate_limiter.py` (default `max_wait` 20 s; `acquire`, which also covers the fail-closed
  `try_acquire`, plus `would_block`, which was True forever for an oversized weight), `analysis-intelligence-service/
  fundamental/rate_limiter.py` and `notification-scheduler-service/scheduler/rate_limiter.py` (`acquire`), and
  `api-gateway/redis_rate_limit.py` (`acquire`, `allow`, `wait_budget_sec`; `main.py::_cb_get` uses the last two to
  decide on CircuitOpenError). An oversized weight now means "the whole bucket": it waits only for the bucket to fill
  (at most `capacity / rps`), drains it, and returns; `allow()` grants it when the bucket is full; `wait_budget_sec()`
  reports the time to fill; `would_block()` is False when the bucket is full. Logged once per call at DEBUG
  ("weight X exceeds bucket capacity Y, treating as Y"). Weight at or below capacity and `capacity == 0` are
  untouched. These copies have no reserve concept, so the clamp is simply `capacity`. Real call site affected:
  `market-data-service/surprise_premarket.py` `rl_acquire("yfinance", weight=len(batch))` (batches of up to 50).
  Tests: 5 existing tests that pinned the stall as "current behaviour" were rewritten (analysis-intelligence
  `test_max_wait_exceeded_proceeds_and_drains_tokens` now reaches the give-up path with an ordinary weight and
  `test_weight_above_burst_capacity_always_stalls_the_full_max_wait` became `..._is_capped_and_does_not_stall`;
  api-gateway `test_redis_rate_limit.py` `test_weight_larger_than_capacity_stalls_until_max_wait`, `test_weighted_allow`
  and `test_weight_forwarded`). New tests: market-data `tests/test_rate_limiter.py` 42 -> 53 (`TestOversizedWeight`);
  analysis-intelligence `tests/test_rate_limiter.py` 89 -> 95; api-gateway `tests/test_redis_rate_limit.py` 71 -> 82;
  NEW FILE `notification-scheduler-service/tests/test_rate_limiter_oversized_weight.py` (9; that service had no bucket
  tests, the file is loaded fresh from its path like `test_rate_limiter_env.py`). On the group 54 code 22 of the new/
  rewritten tests fail (market-data 7, analysis 4, redis 6, notification-scheduler 5); the group 54 versions of the

  `api-gateway/tests/test_rate_limiter.py` (160) all pass; other test files and the other tests in these services were

  api-gateway, market-data-service, analysis-intelligence-service and notification-scheduler-service (`docker compose
  build <service> && docker compose up -d`).
  STILL OPEN: a caller whose weight is above `capacity - reserve` but not above `capacity` is still denied/stalled in the
  gateway's reserve-aware bucket (`test_unreachable_weight_under_reserve_is_denied` pins it as intended); needs your call.
- 2026-10-03 (group 54)  #13 item: `api-gateway/rate_limiter.py::_Bucket.acquire(weight > capacity)` stalled for the
  whole budget on every call. Tokens are capped at `capacity` (`min(capacity, tokens + elapsed * rps)`), so
  `usable >= weight` can never become true for a weight above it. A plain `acquire()` therefore slept out the full
  budget (5 s by default) and then "proceeded anyway", draining the bucket and bumping `denied_events`; `try_acquire()`
  (fail_fast) waited the same budget and then returned False, i.e. the call was skipped on every attempt. The one real
  call site is `surprise_premarket.py::bulk_baselines_from_yfinance`, which does
  `rl_acquire("yfinance", weight=len(batch))` with `SURPRISE_YF_BULK_BATCH=50` against a 6-token bucket. Fixed in
  `acquire()`: when `weight > capacity` the request is treated as a request for everything the caller may take,
  `need = capacity - reserve` (so a background pipeline still leaves the interactive reserve alone). It waits only
  for that to refill (at most `capacity / rps`, e.g. 3 s for yfinance), drains it, and returns. Logged once per call at
  DEBUG. If nothing is attainable (`reserve >= capacity`) behaviour is unchanged, and a weight at or below capacity is
  untouched. The give-up path and its log line still report the caller's original weight. `snapshot()` output is
  unchanged (no new counter). Tests (`tests/test_rate_limiter.py`, 149 -> 160): 11 new in `TestBucketAcquire`
  (full bucket taken immediately, waits for refill not `max_wait`, just-above-capacity, fail_fast granted instead of
  skipped, reserve left intact, reserve + refill wait, DEBUG-only log, still bounded by the budget when nothing refills,
  reserve >= capacity unchanged, weight == capacity not treated as oversized, module `acquire`/`try_acquire`); 8 of
  the 11 fail on the group 53 code, the other 3 guard behaviour that must not change. All 149 existing tests still
  pass unchanged on the new code. Sandbox has no pytest and no network, so this round used a small stand-in runner
  that supports fixtures (autouse/yield), `parametrize`, `raises` and `monkeypatch`: the whole file runs 160/160 on the
  fix, 152 pass / 8 fail on the old code. The rest of the gateway suite was NOT re-run (7530 passed in group 51); the
  other test files that mention the limiter stub it. Run `bash run_tests.sh` on the VM. NEEDS REBUILD:
  `docker compose build api-gateway && docker compose up -d`.
  STILL OPEN, same flaw, NOT changed: (1) the sibling copies of this bucket in market-data-service/rate_limiter.py,
  analysis-intelligence-service/fundamental/rate_limiter.py and notification-scheduler-service/scheduler/rate_limiter.py
  (default `max_wait` 20 s, and market-data-service/surprise_premarket.py also calls
  `rl_acquire("yfinance", weight=len(batch))`), plus `api-gateway/redis_rate_limit.py`'s `_Bucket` (acquire / allow /
  wait_budget_sec). (2) A background caller whose weight is above `capacity - reserve` but not above `capacity` is
  still denied/stalled: `test_unreachable_weight_under_reserve_is_denied` pins that as intended, so I left it for your
  call.
- 2026-10-03 (group 53)  #13 item: `api-gateway/json_safe.py::sanitize` left `np.bool_` and `set` / `frozenset`
  unchanged. `sanitize()` is what `_safe_json_response` runs every payload through before Starlette's
  `json.dumps(..., allow_nan=False)`; anything it returns that `json.dumps` cannot encode becomes a 500 for the
  whole response. `np.bool_` is not a `bool` subclass and was not in the numpy branch (which only handled
  floating / integer / ndarray), so a flag such as `np.float64(3) > np.float64(2)` in a row raised
  `TypeError: Object of type bool is not JSON serializable`; a `set` value (tag sets, id sets) raised
  `Object of type set is not JSON serializable`. Fixed: `np.bool_` -> `bool(obj)` in the numpy branch; `set` /
  `frozenset` -> list, sorted for a stable wire order (falls back to iteration order when members are not mutually
  orderable, e.g. `{1, "a", None}`), members sanitised recursively (so a NaN member becomes null and numpy ints
  become ints). Arrays of bools already worked via `tolist()`. Only `json_safe.py` changed; the training service's
  own `json_safe.py` already handled both and is untouched. Tests (`tests/test_json_safe.py`, 32 -> 42): new
  `TestNumpyBool` (4) and `TestSets` (6); 10 of the 42 fail on the group 52 code. Sandbox has no pytest this round
  (and the network is off), so the file was run through a minimal pytest shim (parametrize / approx /
  monkeypatch.setattr only): 42/42 pass on the fix, 32 pass / 10 fail on the old code. The whole gateway suite was
  NOT re-run (7530 passed in group 51); `test_main_core` / `test_main_surprise_routes` only reference `json_safe`
  through stubs, so nothing else pins the old behaviour. Run `bash run_tests.sh` on the VM. NEEDS REBUILD:
  `docker compose build api-gateway && docker compose up -d`.
- 2026-10-03 (group 52)  #13 item: `api-gateway/price_resolver.py::_as_positive_float` accepted `+inf`. The guard
  was `px <= 0 or px != px`, which rejects zero, negatives and NaN but lets `+inf` through (also the strings
  `"inf"`, `"Infinity"` and `"1e400"`, which `float()` turns into inf). `extract_safe_price` / `resolve_display_price`
  / `apply_price_aliases` / `ensure_row_price` would then stamp `inf` into close/price/cmp/current_price/ltp/
  last_price/prev_close, and from there into scoring, % change and order sizing. The guard is now
  `not math.isfinite(px) or px <= 0`, so an infinite value is skipped like any other bad one: the resolver falls
  through to the next key / source, and returns 0.0 (or leaves the row untouched) when nothing finite is available.
  The old test `test_infinity_is_accepted_as_positive` pinned the bug and is replaced by
  `test_infinity_and_nan_rejected` (+/-inf, inf strings, 1e400, nan) and `test_large_finite_still_accepted`; new
  class `TestInfinityNeverResolves` (8 tests) covers fall-through across tick/decision/feed/nested feed, the
  only-inf-available case, `apply_price_aliases` (inf price keeps an existing positive value; inf `prev_close` is
  replaced) and `ensure_row_price`. 13 of the 72 tests in `tests/test_price_resolver.py` fail on the group 51 code.
  Sandbox has no pytest this round, so this file was run through a minimal pytest shim (parametrize/approx only):

- 2026-10-03 (group 50)  #13 item: `api-gateway/batch_worker.py::run_in_batches` dropped a whole batch from the
  totals when `asyncio.gather` itself failed. The worker gather uses `return_exceptions=True`, so a worker error
  never reaches the `except`; what does is a failure of gather itself. That branch set `raw = []` after cancelling
  the tasks, and `zip(to_fetch, raw)` then skipped every item of the batch: not counted in `processed`, not in
  `results`, not in `errors`. The scan's universe total silently came up short, the batch's progress callback
  reported the old `processed` figure, and `main.py`'s scan summary (which reads `processed`/`errors`) never
  mentioned those symbols. The branch now rebuilds one entry per item after the cancel: a task that had already
  finished keeps its real result or its own exception, and one that was cancelled (or would not stop within
  `_cancel_tasks`' 2 s) is recorded as failed with the gather error (`{"item": ..., "error": "RuntimeError:
  boom"}`), through the same per-item path as any other failure, so `collect_errors_from_exceptions=False` still
  suppresses the error entry but the item is still counted. Invariant now tested: `processed == len(results) +
  len(errors)` for a batch whose gather failed. Tests (`tests/test_batch_worker.py`, 75 total): the old
  `test_gather_failure_cancels_tasks_and_moves_on` pinned the bug (`processed == 1`, item "neither processed nor
  recorded") and now asserts `processed == 2` and the recorded error; 5 new tests cover a full 5-item batch, the
  progress callback, a mix of finished / failed / cancelled workers, error collection off, and a failed batch not
  touching the next one. 6 of the 75 fail on the group 49 code; module coverage stays 100% / 100% (130
  statements, 54 branches); 3 repeated runs green. Sandbox: `tests/test_batch_worker.py` run directly. Not
  changed: a gather failure is only reachable in practice by an outer cancellation / interpreter-level fault, so
  this is a correctness-of-accounting fix, not a fix for a failure seen in production. NEEDS REBUILD:
  `docker compose build api-gateway && docker compose up -d`.

  90 s `trades:` report cache is deliberately memory-only, and no file outside `api-gateway` references a

  every service carry prefixes it never uses. The risk is silent drift: a prefix missing from a copy does not raise,
  `_is_durable()` just returns False and the key becomes memory-only until the next restart. New
  `api-gateway/tests/test_kv_cache_drift.py` (59 tests, reads the copies as source, no imports) pins: all six copies

- 2026-10-03 (group 42)  `surprise_premarket.bulk_baselines_from_yfinance` skipped its inter-batch pause after a
  failed or empty batch. The `time.sleep(YF_BULK_BATCH_PAUSE)` sat at the bottom of the loop, after the
  `continue`s for a raised `yf.download()` and for a None/empty frame, so exactly the batches that most likely hit
  a 429 got no pause before the next call. The pause now sits at the top of the loop (`if i > 0`), so every batch
  after the first is preceded by one whatever happened to the previous batch; successful runs sleep the same
  number of times as before (batches - 1) and a single batch never sleeps. Fixed in both copies

  elsewhere, only this loop was changed). api-gateway `tests/test_surprise_premarket.py`:

- 2026-10-03 (group 36)  `oracle_compat.exec_ddl_safe` (all 8 byte-identical copies, kept identical): a real
  DDL failure (anything other than "already exists" / the benign ORA codes) used to be logged at DEBUG
  only, and callers then logged "ensured index ..." anyway, so a failed index/column was invisible.
  Now it returns a bool (True = ran or already there, False = genuinely failed), logs
  `exec_ddl_safe FAILED (<dialect>): <error> [sql: <first 100 chars>]` at WARNING, and still never
  raises (startup must not die on one index). SQLite's "duplicate column name" is also treated as
  benign. The three "ensured index" callers (real-trade-service `_ensure_hot_path_indexes` and
  `_ensure_nextday_watchlist_indexes`, position-stocks-service `_ensure_hot_path_indexes`) log
  "could NOT ensure index ..." at WARNING on False; a stub returning None still counts as success, so
  existing monkeypatched tests are unaffected. The other callers (kv_cache, hotpicks/ipo/surprise
  schema, angelone_ws_feed) ignore the result and now get the WARNING for free. Old pin
  `test_a_real_ddl_error_is_swallowed_by_exec_ddl_safe_but_still_reported_as_ensured` rewritten
  (both ensurers); DEBUG-level expectations in the api-gateway / analysis-intelligence / position-stocks
  oracle_compat tests changed to WARNING; new tests for the return value, SQL-in-log, duplicate-column
  and the three callers. Sandbox: oracle_compat tests api-gateway 108, analysis-intelligence 108,
  market-data 39, position-stocks 110 (+2 skipped) passed; real-trade 4 pre-existing failures (oracledb
  not installed, identical on the group 35 upload); test_db real-trade 225 / position-stocks 49;
  api-gateway kv_cache + hotpicks/ipo/surprise schema 548; analysis-intelligence kv_cache 239. The new
  tests fail against the old oracle_compat. NEEDS REBUILD: all services that bundle oracle_compat
  (`docker compose build` then `up -d`); expect WARNING lines only if a DDL genuinely fails.
- 2026-10-03 (group 35) — `notifier.py` in real-trade-service and position-stocks-service: a FAILED
  delivery no longer eats the 5-minute dedup window. `_should_send` still reserves the slot before
  delivery (keeps simultaneous identical sends race-free), but when every channel fails the slot is
  shortened to `_DEDUP_RETRY_AFTER_FAIL_S` (30s), so the same alert (SELL failed / exit blocked) is
  retried after 30s instead of being reported "sent" and never attempted for 5 min. A call
  suppressed during that back-off now returns False (was True). A hard outage still cannot storm:
  one real attempt per 30s per message. Covers notify_sync, notify_async, notify_fire_and_forget
  (incl. a crash inside the background thread). Old pin `test_a_failed_delivery_still_consumes_the_dedup_slot`
  rewritten + 9 new tests (real-trade) and 6 (position-stocks). notifier.py 100% in both.
  real-trade test_notifier 81 passed; position-stocks full suite 2339 passed. Unrelated sandbox
  failures (oracledb/dhanhq not installed) fail identically on the original upload. NEEDS REBUILD:

- 2026-10-02 (group 31) — api-gateway `main.py` `/ops/check-alert`: added a per-process cool-down.
  The same problem set (same open-circuit set / error-rate condition) is notified at most once per
  `OPS_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SEC` (default 900; 0 disables; bad value -> 900). A new problem alerts
  immediately, recovery clears the state, and the cool-down is only recorded after a SUCCESSFUL
  delivery (a failed send retries next call). Suppressed calls return `alerted: false,
  suppressed: true, cooldown_remaining_sec`. State is in-memory, so a restart can repeat one alert.
  Pin in `test_main_ops_routes.py` rewritten + 7 new tests. `bash run_tests.sh` for api-gateway:
  exit 0, 100% coverage.

  `asyncio.Lock` bound to whichever event loop touched it first, crashing the
  ws-feed background thread for good on any cross-loop contention; fixed with
  a per-event-loop lock (then a follow-up fix, 82b, to stop it leaking memory
  via one-shot `asyncio.run()` loops using a `WeakKeyDictionary`)

Tests: `test_group421_exit_reason_and_hold.py`, `scripts/test_hold_bucket_check.py`, sniper tests updated. Details: `docs/GROUP421_HOLD_LABEL_EXIT_REASON_HOLD_BUCKETS.md`.
- group422: record-only path for the decision structure policy (item 3 of the 16 Oct review; defaults unchanged, nothing loosens). Group 370 removed shadow mode, so a rule could only be live or the whole policy off. `STRUCTURE_POLICY_MODE=record` evaluates every rule and applies none; `STRUCTURE_POLICY_SHADOW_RULES=P7,P8` makes just those rules (id or `_`-boundary prefix) record-only while the rest stay live. Old `shadow` / `caps` values still mean live. The `structure` block gains `shadow_rules` + `would_be_decision`; a record-only rule that fired also blocks a P9 upgrade (an upgrade only runs in full mode) and record mode leaves legacy stop / target alone. Observation hook stores `shadow:<id>` rules + `features.would_be` so a report can compare would-be vs actual (`structure_obs` whitelists `would_be`). Frontend `policyRecord` / snapshot / tally show record-only rules as "shadow, not applied". Files: decision/structure_policy.py, structure_obs_hook.py, training/structure_obs.py, frontend structureView.ts(+test), docker-compose.yml, .env.example, ENV_REFERENCE.md, tests/test_group422_policy_record_mode.py. See docs/GROUP422_POLICY_RECORD_MODE.md.
- group423: independent-groups count that does not double-count (item 2 of the 16 Oct review; OFF by default, nothing changes live). The five TQS groups overlap: `location` re-reads zone / fib / support / room that `zones` already saw, and `risk` comes from the same stop / target as room, so P7's "2 groups" was often one piece of evidence counted twice. Engine (shared + 5 vendored copies, synced) adds `independent_groups_strict` (0..3 = structure, entry zone = max(zones, location), liquidity; risk dropped because P4 gates R:R itself) -> summary `ms_groups_strict`; the existing `independent_groups_positive` / `ms_groups` / TQS are unchanged. Policy rule `P7X_INDEP` (BUY NOW -> PREPARE) fires only when `groups_strict_min` > 0 (default 0; `STRUCTURE_POLICY_PARAMS_JSON={"groups_strict_min":2}`); a missing value trips it when on (unknown is never bullish). Run it record-only with `STRUCTURE_POLICY_SHADOW_RULES=P7X` (group422) and read `shadow:P7X_INDEP` / `features.groups_strict` from the observations; P7 does not cover P7X. Golden file regenerated (only the new key differs). Files: shared/market_structure/{scoring,engine,summary}.py (+vendored), decision/structure_policy.py, structure_obs_hook.py, training/structure_obs.py, frontend structureView.ts, tests. See docs/GROUP423_STRICT_INDEPENDENT_GROUPS.md.
- group424: four fixes from the 18 Oct review (nothing changes in live trading decisions). (1) `scripts/structure_gate_check.py`: the drop-one note was inverted - `drop_one_edge_pp` is candidate minus the gate-without-the-rule, so the rules the gate does better WITHOUT are the NEGATIVE ones; it had named the helpful rule (`bearish`, +0.07) and missed the harmful one (`rr`, -0.19). Now flags `drop_one_edge_pp < -0.05` (n >= 30); test fixed and a regression test added in `scripts/test_structure_gate_check.py`. (2) api-gateway `structure_refresh.py`: a symbol whose structure fetch keeps failing (new listing, technical answers 503) was re-selected on every catch-up pass because failed fetches left no trace (lifetime 19 failures of 24). A failure now stamps `ms_failed_at` / `ms_fail_n` on the feed row (old structure keys kept; never creates a row), logs a WARNING naming the symbol, and backs the symbol off 1 h, 2 h, 4 h ... 24 h. A later answered attempt (newer `ms_refreshed_at`) or `force` lifts it; a REFRESHED or UNCHANGED result also drops the stamp (both keys are in `OWNED_KEYS`). 12 new tests in `tests/test_group360_structure_refresh.py` + one existing assertion updated. (3) real-trade `tests/test_group294_late_close_refresh.py` no longer depends on the clock: `now + 25 h` is the day after tomorrow in IST between 23:00 and 24:00, so it is pinned to 01:00 IST on the next IST day. (4) Docs: `ms_engine_switch.py` docstring and `docs/GROUP339_MARKET_STRUCTURE_ENGINE.md` now say `BOARD_USE_MS_ENGINE` defaults to 1 (config.py), P9 is off by default since group 415, and the A/B evidence section lists seeds 7 and 11 (G2 not met).
- group424b: structure R:R floor OFF by default (the one rule whose replay edge did not hold: drop-one +0.39 on seed 7, -0.19 on seed 11; `chase`, `bearish`, `stop_wide` were non-negative on both seeds and stay live). `STRUCTURE_MIN_RR` default 1.2 -> 0 in real-trade `config.py` and api-gateway `buy_sniper.py` / `sniper_observation.py`; decision policy `rr_min` 1.2 -> 0 with a `> 0` guard, so only the ratio test of `P4_RR` is off - its `no_target` and `stop_too_wide` checks, `P4_REWARD`, chase, stretch, room, trend and BOS rules are unchanged. THIS LOOSENS LIVE GATING (entries with structure R:R below 1.2 are no longer refused by structure). Rollback with no code change: `STRUCTURE_MIN_RR=1.2` (real-trade + sniper) and `STRUCTURE_POLICY_PARAMS_JSON={"rr_min":1.2}` (decision). A value in the VM `.env` still overrides the default. `scripts/calibration_pack.py` now reports the rr default as 0. New `decision/tests/test_group424_rr_floor_off.py` (4), new sniper test for the floor off / env back on, sniper rule test no longer assumes 1.2, calibration-pack test text updated. Docs: `.env.example`, `docs/GROUP339_*.md`.
- group427: order-flow record on every entry (nothing changes in live decisions) + three fixes from the 18 Oct VM run. (1) `trade_decisions.flow_json` (real-trade) and `scalp_positions.flow_json` (position-stocks), additive nullable columns: spread, best-5 bid/ask quantity + imbalance, book value, price vs VWAP / open, day position and, for approved entries, the 20-level book (`ORDER_FLOW_RECORD`, `ORDER_FLOW_DEPTH20`); `/records` gains by_imbalance_5 / by_imbalance_20 / by_spread. (2) the backtests print progress (`scripts/_progress.py`) so a long run does not look hung; `fetch_history_for_backtest.py --benchmark-only` adds the missing `^NSEI.json`. (3) `scripts/vm_docker_recover.sh` for the "parent snapshot does not exist" image-store error. See docs/GROUP427_ORDER_FLOW_RECORD.md.
- group428: outlier-proof checks for `scripts/entry_rules_backtest.py` and `scripts/legacy_gate_backtest.py` (tooling only, nothing changes in live trading). The 18 Oct entry-rules report showed +15.8 % for pre-holiday entries and +17.8 % for monthly expiry, which is data (unadjusted splits / bad candles) plus market-wide day clustering the (symbol x month) bootstrap cannot see. New `scripts/_robust.py`: median edge, clipped (1st-99th percentile) edge, a day-cluster bootstrap, extreme-row count and a `verdict_robust` that needs all three to agree; Part 1b repeats Part 1 without entries whose window holds a >35 % one-day move; stop/target cells need a difference of at least `--min-effect-pp` (0.05) to be `BETTER` / `WORSE` (smaller = `better_tiny` / `worse_tiny`). See docs/GROUP428_ROBUST_BACKTEST_CHECKS.md.
- group429: fixed the one failing real-trade test, `test_group425_kronos_entry_wiring::test_gate_5_6_enforce_waits_on_cost_model` (test only, no service code changed). Cause: group 266 prices a same-day CNC round trip like MIS, so a 0.4 % forecast target is 3.7x the cost, above the test's 3x floor and correctly let through by Gate 5.6; not clock dependent (checked at 10:30 and 16:00 IST). Both Gate 5.6 tests now use a 10x floor so they do not depend on the exact charge constants. real-trade 4939 passed, 0 failed. See docs/GROUP429_KRONOS_WIRING_TEST_FIX.md.
- group430: `scripts/records_report.py` (one-command closed-trade report: medians, best-trade and outlier-free totals, stop overshoot, late eod_squareoff list, order-flow buckets from group 427, `--since` / `--until`, `(few)` marks) replacing the throwaway rt_split.py; CI now runs `test_entry_rules_backtest.py`, `test_robust_backtest.py`, `test_records_report.py` and watches their scripts (the first two were never in the workflow). Tooling only, no service code changed. See docs/GROUP430_RECORDS_REPORT.md.
- group431: `scripts/export_records.py` (one-command fresh export of closed-trade records: admin login or TOKEN, 60-day clamp, prints newest/oldest closed date, truncation warning) because the 18 Oct `records_report.py --since 2026-10-01` saw only 1 trade (stale export); `records_report.py` now warns on a stale export, separates stops that lost MORE than the initial stop from trailed stops that closed inside it (the old line misreported a trailed stop as a -2.69 overshoot), and hides the charges share below `--min-n` trades. CI covers the new script. Tooling only. See docs/GROUP431_EXPORT_RECORDS.md.
- group432: merge of the group 430 zip (group 426 changes re-applied; those were missing from the group 429 zip) with the group 431 zip (records_report / export_records tooling). Numbering note: the earlier "group430" label on the re-apply zip clashed with this changelog's group430 (records_report), so the re-apply is renamed group432 here. Content, no trading decision changes: (1) Refill Additional Data counts symbols whose fundamentals call returned nothing (`fund_missing`, one WARNING) instead of ending "587/587 ok, 0 errors", and no longer relabels a priced row's `source`. (2) `start_bulk_feed` also prices the feed index on top of the scan universe; `BULK_FEED_INCLUDE_INDEX=0` restores the old list. Doc: docs/GROUP438_IMPORT_RACE.md. Tests: tests/test_group438_import_race.py (the 6 pure ones ran under a stand-in; the 5 SQLite ones are unrun, no sqlalchemy in the sandbox).

Reporting only, nothing live changes. Doc: docs/GROUP439_52W_REGIME_SPLIT.md. Tests: 4 new in scripts/test_legacy_gate_backtest.py (17 pass).

- group441: the five items that three earlier sessions built but never packaged (the zips from groups 436 / 437 / 439 did not contain them; the work was recovered from the session log and re-verified). Numbering note: the build log called this "group437", which clashes with the compose-duplicate-limits entry above, so everything is labelled group441 here. NO trading decision changes and nothing is switched on. (1) `policy_flags` / `ms_ab_report` replay rule `p7x_indep` (default off, same semantics as the live P7X) and `scripts/p8_retest_evidence.py` (does the P8 retest / CHOCH branch earn its place: rescued vs blocked vs clean days, cluster bootstrap, verdict `retest_ok | retest_hurts | no_edge | insufficient_sample | no_data`). NEW BEHAVIOUR ON DEPLOY: the recorder is ON by default and writes small JSON files to a docker volume; `BAR_RECORD_ENABLED=false` switches it off. (3) `scripts/ml_v2_walkforward.py`: offline, embargoed walk-forward of the FEATURE_COLUMNS_V2 features (stdlib ridge, AUC vs `ms_tqs`, versioned report); it never touches the live model. - group447: calibration-runner-service. (1) a Telegram message (via notification-scheduler-service, channel telegram) when an AUTOMATIC run finishes or is blocked by the market-hours guard (`notify.py`; `RUNNER_NOTIFY_ENABLED`, `RUNNER_NOTIFY_MANUAL`; a failed message only leaves a note, never changes the run). (2) `state.last_automatic` / `last_automatic_by_pack` kept in `state/last_automatic.json`, shown as a timestamp chip on every sub-tab and on the timetable. (3) `RUNNER_KEEP_LATEST_ONLY` (default true): a new result of a job / pack deletes the older runs of it (blocked / cancelled never replace a real result). New `frontend/src/alignment.css` (loads last): (1) desktop top bar no longer shows under the mobile header on phones (`.app-topbar { display: flex }` in index.css beat Tailwind `hidden md:flex` at every width); (2) sidebar footer "Search & commands / Ctrl K" stays on one line; (3) sidebar rows tighten by screen height so all pages + footer fit on 1366x768 laptops without scrolling; (4) TV sidebar denser with a soft scroll fade; (5) skip link slides out by its own height (peeked onto 4K screens); (6) dashboard hero title keeps its width, market clock wraps below on phones; (7) Paper Trades double inset removed so it lines up with its page title; (8) heading rows wrap/balance. - group 466 (runner/backtest) (2026-10-19): calibration-runner-service + backtest scripts. (1) heavy jobs get a 6 h limit (`RUNNER_HEAVY_TIMEOUT_S`, floor 1 h, default 21600; `jobs.HEAVY_S`) after the 355-symbol A/B replay was killed at 2 h with 290 done. (2) per-symbol checkpoint / resume: `scripts/_progress.run_jobs(ckpt=, resume=, stamp=)`, `--checkpoint` / `--resume` in ms_ab_report, p8_retest_evidence, legacy_gate_backtest, entry_rules_backtest, ml_v2_walkforward, `vm_calibrate.sh`, `jobs._ck`; catalog flag `resumable`; **Resume** button in Runs and downloads and the run window (`calibrationApi.start(..., resume)`, `POST /runs {resume}`). (3) the child runs in its own process group and a timeout / cancel / shutdown kills the whole group (pool workers no longer survive). (4) Telegram "URLError" fix: `notify.Outbox` + `start_outbox_flusher` keep an undelivered message on disk and retry it (`RUNNER_OUTBOX_RETRY_S`, `RUNNER_OUTBOX_MAX_AGE_S`), the run note says it will be sent again, and the message has a `What failed:` list. (5) automatic runs wait for the stack's `/health` first (`RUNNER_PREFLIGHT_S`). Not the same as the frontend's close-all-and-disarm "group 466". Doc: docs/GROUP466_RUNNER_TIMEOUT_RESUME_OUTBOX.md. Tests: 5 new (pure parts run; evaluate_mode wiring unrun in sandbox).

- group 472 (2026-10-20): real-trade `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` (default 3, REAL only, 0 = off): an entry whose risk-sized quantity is below it is WAITed (gate_tag `min_qty`) before the cost gate. TIGHTENS entries; rollback `ENTRY_MIN_QTY=0`. See `docs/GROUP472_MIN_QTY.md`.

- group 473 (2026-10-20): real-trade `GET /review-fixes/status` (read-only, admin for REAL): effective settings of groups 470-472 (overnight hold, 14:00 cut-off and whether it blocks now, overshoot allowance, min qty, BROKER_STOP_ENABLED), today's losing stop-outs + symbols + pause state, opening-range shadow counters. Use it on the VM to confirm the new defaults really are in force. No trading change. Not run here (no fastapi/sqlalchemy in sandbox); compile-checked only.

- group 474 (2026-10-20): real-trade `trade_records.py`: every record now carries `orb_would_enter`, `orb_reasons`, `orb_range_pct` (from the group 470 opening-range shadow verdict in `flow_json["orb"]`), and the expectancy report has a `by_orb_shadow` group (`orb_would_enter` / `orb_would_skip:<reason>` / `no_verdict`). This is how the shadow idea is tested on your own closed trades before any live use. Record/report only, no trading change. Tests: `tests/test_group474_orb_records.py` (2; the helpers were also run stand-alone because the sandbox has no sqlalchemy).

- group 475 (2026-10-20): `scripts/env_review_check.py` (+ `scripts/test_env_review_check.py`, 4 tests, run and passing): read-only check of the VM `.env` for values that undo the group 470-472 fixes (`OVERNIGHT_HOLD_ENABLED=true`, blank / later `ENTRY_LAST_TIME_IST`, `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` below 3, `STOP_OVERSHOOT_ALLOWANCE_PCT=0`, stop-out guard off, `BROKER_STOP_ENABLED=false`); labels LOOSENS / TIGHTENS / OVERRIDE, `--strict` exits 1. Run on the VM: `python3 scripts/env_review_check.py ~/stockky-v2/.env --strict`. No service code touched.
- group 476 (2026-10-21): auto-calibration. New `services/calibration-runner-service/autocalib.py`: every switch that has a test is set from the latest test result (hold / tighten at once / loosen after two different runs), applied via /control/flags, one Telegram message per evaluation, pins + master switch + dry run, page section "Auto-calibration". Also fixed the 10 CI failures (close_all silent excepts; approve-first test leaked a fake cache). `docs/GROUP476_AUTO_CALIBRATION.md`. Tests: `tests/test_autocalib.py` (15).
- group 477 (2026-10-21): Real Money Calibration page caught up with groups 470-472: fixed OVERNIGHT_HOLD_ENABLED shipped default (true -> false), added STOPOUT_GUARD_ENABLED / STOPOUT_DAY_PAUSE_N / STOPOUT_SYMBOL_BLOCK / ENTRY_MIN_QTY / STOP_OVERSHOOT_ALLOWANCE_PCT / ORB_MODE switches (protected from auto-calibration); auto-calibration never turns overnight hold ON; new test_group477_catalog_matches_config.py (both services); CI runs test_env_review_check.py. `docs/GROUP476_AUTO_CALIBRATION.md`.
- group 478 (2026-10-21): fix: `services/calibration-runner-service/Dockerfile` did not COPY `autocalib.py` (group 476), so `main.py` `import autocalib` raised ModuleNotFoundError at startup and the container would not come up. Added it to the COPY list; new guard test `tests/test_group478_dockerfile_copies_modules.py` (every service .py is copied, every local import is a copied module; negative control checked) and a CI line. Verified by importing and starting `main` in an image-like layout. Rebuild: `docker compose build calibration-runner-service && docker compose up -d calibration-runner-service`.
- group 479 (2026-10-21): position-stocks-service parity with real-trade groups 470-472: `SCALP_ENTRY_CUTOFF_IST` default 14:00 (was blank), whole-day stop-out guard (`orders/stopout_guard.py`, `STOPOUT_*`), `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` 3, opening-range recorder (`orders/orb_shadow.py`, `ORB_MODE=shadow`, record only), `GET /review-fixes/status`; real-trade `BROKER_STOP_INTRADAY` (default off): resting Dhan stop for a same-day INTRADAY position (needs `ENTRY_PRODUCT_MODE=auto`, not verified live). TIGHTENS position-stocks entries; rollbacks in `docs/GROUP479_POSITION_STOCKS_PARITY_INTRADAY_STOP.md`. Tests: 16 + 5 new; pure parts run under a stand-in runner, the DB-backed entry path and the real broker call were not run here (no pytest/sqlalchemy/fastapi in sandbox).
- group 480 (2026-10-21): position-stocks entry cost gate now counts Dhan's percentage brokerage (min(Rs 20, 0.03 %) per leg; `SCALP_COST_GATE_BROKERAGE_PCT` 0.03, `_CAP_RS` 20; 0 = old Rs 0) - it priced brokerage at Rs 0 and under-counted the round trip by about 0.06 %; new read-only `scripts/charges_split.py` (same-day vs carried trades, entry-value buckets) with tests and CI wiring. No larger sizing, no leverage, no live opening-range entry (needs evidence). See docs/GROUP480_COST_GATE_BROKERAGE_CHARGES_SPLIT.md.
- group 481 (2026-10-21): real-trade evidence-gated size ladder (`entry_engine/size_ladder.py`, `SIZE_LADDER_*`): the automatic REAL per-trade cap grows 25 % per level (max 2) only after 10 / 20 captured days of positive stored net P&L with 60 % positive days, and drops to the base when the newest 3 days lose; stays at the base cap with current results; shown in `/review-fixes/status`; protected from auto-calibration. See docs/GROUP481_SIZE_LADDER.md.
- group 482 (2026-10-21): read-only `scripts/vm_review_evidence.sh` - one command on the VM that gathers review settings, both `/review-fixes/status` views, the charges ledger by component, `charges_split.py` / `records_report.py` on a fresh REAL export and `gate_scorecard.py`; tests added and wired into CI; 482b: the script can log in itself (`ADMIN_USERNAME`, silent password prompt), skips the 401 call when it has no token, no longer cuts the gate table off, and `gate_scorecard.py` splits its no-bars count into no-folder days vs unrecorded symbols. No trading code changed. See docs/GROUP482_VM_REVIEW_EVIDENCE.md.
- group 482c (2026-10-21): real-trade `/review-fixes/status`, `/kronos/status`, `/range52w/status`, `/timesfm/status` returned 422 (`query.mode` required: `require_admin_if_real` on a route with no `{mode}`); they use `require_admin` now, with a source-level guard test. First full evidence run: 72 % of 60-day charges were DP on carried trades entered after 15:00 (already stopped by the 14:00 cut-off of group 470). See docs/GROUP482_VM_REVIEW_EVIDENCE.md.

- A3 (2026-10-22): write-behind queue for evidence-only rows in real-trade (`write_behind.py`). HOLD `trade_exit_decisions` rows (the insert that hit DPY-4024 inside the stop loop) and plain WAIT `trade_decisions` rows (one per candidate, previously in the same transaction as orders) are queued and written by a background worker in batches; a DB timeout in any tick pauses it for `WRITE_BEHIND_BACKOFF_S` (group 347's back-off, whole cycle). Orders, fills, positions, cash, non-HOLD exit decisions and audit rows stay synchronous. Bounded (oldest dropped), `WRITE_BEHIND_ENABLED=0` restores inline writes, `GET /write-behind/status`, drained at shutdown. Live quotes needed no change (market-data's writer is already change-only / priority-first / backed off; real-trade only reads). See `docs/A3_WRITE_BEHIND_QUEUE.md`.
- A13 (2026-10-22): (1) hourly read-only broker-vs-book drift check in real-trade (`execution/hourly_reconcile.py`, run from the fast tick, `GET /reconcile-report/REAL[?run=true]`): warns on a book position the broker does not hold, a broker qty below the book, and REAL orders stuck PLACED / PARTIAL > 90 min; one Telegram message when the drift set changes (repeat every 4 h, one "agree again"). It changes nothing in the book. `HOURLY_RECONCILE_INTERVAL_MINUTES` (60, 0 = off), `HOURLY_RECONCILE_STALE_ORDER_MINUTES` (90). (2) trade records: `source_tab` / `decision_label` / `conviction` now fall back position -> entry order -> candidate (manual entries labelled `manual`), with `attribution_source`; `expectancy_report` has `attribution_coverage`. No new column, so history is repaired on read; `stop_basis` / order-flow stay empty on trades entered before groups 396 / 427. See `docs/A13_HOURLY_RECONCILE_AND_ATTRIBUTION.md`.
- A11 (2026-10-22): account-wide daily loss cap across both engines. New table `stockky_shared_daily_pnl` + `shared_daily_pnl.py` in each service; real-trade `risk_engine` check 3a (`account_daily_loss_limit`) and position-stocks `ledger.reserve_capital()` block new BUYs when the combined net loss of both engines reaches `ACCOUNT_MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT` (default 3.0, 0 = off). Exits and the per-service caps are unchanged. Also regenerated `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.md` (it was already out of date, which fails the CI check). See `docs/A11_ACCOUNT_DAILY_LOSS_CAP.md`.
- A4b (2026-10-22): one admin-session secret per service (`session_keys.py`, identical copy in real-trade, position-stocks, api-gateway, calibration-runner). New env `SESSION_SECRET_REAL_TRADE` / `SESSION_SECRET_POSITION_STOCKS` / `SESSION_SECRET_CALIBRATION_RUNNER`; tokens carry a `kid`; each service accepts only its fixed kid list; legacy `SESSION_SECRET` still works until removed. Doc: `docs/A4B_PER_SERVICE_SECRETS.md`.
- C18-C20 (2026-10-22): `shared/market_structure/patterns.py` (context only, vendored by the sync script): 16 candlestick + 12 chart patterns, Bollinger squeeze, golden / death cross, MACD+RSI confluence, support / resistance flip; returned as `patterns` by `analyze_extras()` (`/structure/{sym}?extras=1`). Nothing reads it for decisions. C21 (scoring / ML columns) deliberately not done. Doc: `docs/C18_C20_PATTERNS.md`.
- D22-D24 (2026-10-22): offline ML groundwork in `decision-prediction-service/training/`: `triple_barrier.py` (cost-aware target / stop / timeout labels, stop wins ties, purge helper), `targets.py` (`TripleBarrier_Net` / `TripleBarrier_Class`, default unchanged), `calibration.py` (Brier, reliability / ECE, isotonic, MA-crossover baseline comparison with the 15-trade floor), `pit_universe.py` (bhavcopy-archive universe that keeps delisted symbols, membership from prior bars only), `fo_features.py` (F&O ban flags, OI build-up features). No live behaviour changed; not yet wired into `train.py`. Tests: 19 new (run, passing, plain runner); the parquet read branch was not run (no pyarrow). See `docs/D22_D24_ML_LABELS_UNIVERSE_FO.md`.
- RR 1:2 (2026-10-22): position-stocks `ENTRY_MIN_REWARD_RISK` (default 2.0, same name as real-trade, 0 = off) skips an automatic entry whose final target / stop is under 2:1 (`orders/rr_floor.py`, tag `RR_FLOOR`); `ADAPTIVE_BAR_TARGET_RR` 1.8 -> 2.0, `SCALP_TARGET_CAP_MIN_RR` 1.5 -> 2.0, bar-level floor 1.2 -> the entry floor. real-trade already enforced 2:1 (unchanged; `STRUCTURE_MIN_RR` stays off). TIGHTENS position-stocks entries. Calibration page flag + protected from auto-calibration. See `docs/RR_FLOOR_1_2_2026-10-22.md`. Tests: 7 new (run); existing position-stocks suites not run here.
